# src/pik2video/gui/common/cursor_manager.py
======================================================================

# src/pik2video/gui/common/cursor_manager.py
"""
Единый менеджер курсора для прозрачных окон без фокуса.

Проблема macOS:
- setCursor не применяется на окнах без key window;
- QApplication.setOverrideCursor() тоже не работает для окон с
  Qt.WindowDoesNotAcceptFocus (macOS не шлёт cursorUpdate такому окну);
- topLevelAt() не видит прозрачные окна.

Решение: NSCursor через PyObjC — системный курсор, применяется глобально.
Вызываем NSCursor.<shape>().set() на каждом tick (30 Гц), потому что
система сбрасывает курсор при движении мыши.

Приоритет:
1. Мышь над _blocked (окно пресетов, окно записи) → обычный Arrow.
2. Мышь над зарегистрированным окном (_watched) → спросить cursor_for_position.
3. Иначе → Arrow.
"""

import logging

from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, Qt, QTimer
from PySide6.QtGui import QCursor
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication, QWidget

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


try:
    from AppKit import NSCursor
    NSCURSOR_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    NSCURSOR_AVAILABLE = False
    logger.warning("NSCursor недоступен — курсор будет работать через Qt override")


DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS = 33   # ~30 Гц


_NS_CURSOR_MAP = {
    Qt.ArrowCursor:       "arrowCursor",
    Qt.CrossCursor:       "crosshairCursor",
    Qt.OpenHandCursor:    "openHandCursor",
    Qt.ClosedHandCursor:  "closedHandCursor",
    Qt.PointingHandCursor:"pointingHandCursor",
    Qt.IBeamCursor:       "IBeamCursor",
    Qt.SizeAllCursor:     "openHandCursor",
    Qt.BlankCursor:       "arrowCursor",
}


class CursorManager(QObject):
    """Управляет курсором для окон, которым нужен нестандартный вид.

    Зарегистрированное окно должно реализовать:
        cursor_for_position(pos: QPoint) -> Qt.CursorShape | None
    где pos — координаты в системе этого окна.
    """

    def __init__(self, parent=None, interval_ms: int = DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._interval_ms = interval_ms
        self._current_shape: Qt.CursorShape | None = None
        self._current_ns_name: str | None = None

        self._watched: dict[int, QWidget] = {}
        self._blocked: list[QWidget] = []

        self._timer = QTimer(self)
        self._timer.setInterval(self._interval_ms)
        self._timer.timeout.connect(self._tick)

    # ── API ──

    def register(self, widget: QWidget):
        self._watched[id(widget)] = widget
        if not self._timer.isActive():
            self._timer.start()
        logger.debug(f"CursorManager: register {widget.__class__.__name__}")

    def unregister(self, widget: QWidget):
        self._watched.pop(id(widget), None)
        if not self._watched:
            self._timer.stop()
            self._apply(Qt.ArrowCursor)
            self._current_shape = None
            self._current_ns_name = None
        logger.debug(f"CursorManager: unregister {widget.__class__.__name__}")

    def block(self, widget: QWidget):
        if widget not in self._blocked:
            self._blocked.append(widget)
            logger.debug(f"CursorManager: block {widget.__class__.__name__}")

    def unblock(self, widget: QWidget):
        if widget in self._blocked:
            self._blocked.remove(widget)

    def shutdown(self):
        self._timer.stop()
        self._apply(Qt.ArrowCursor)
        self._watched.clear()
        self._blocked.clear()


    def _tick(self):
        if not self._watched:
            return

        if QApplication.activeModalWidget() is not None:
            self._apply(Qt.ArrowCursor)
            return

        pos = QCursor.pos()

        for b in self._blocked:
            try:
                if b.isVisible() and b.frameGeometry().contains(pos):
                    self._apply(Qt.ArrowCursor)
                    return
            except RuntimeError:
                continue

        for w in list(self._watched.values()):
            try:
                if w.isVisible() and w.frameGeometry().contains(pos):
                    local = w.mapFromGlobal(pos)
                    handler = getattr(w, "cursor_for_position", None)
                    if handler is None:
                        continue
                    shape = handler(local)
                    if shape is None:
                        continue
                    self._apply(shape)
                    return
            except RuntimeError:
                continue

        self._apply(Qt.ArrowCursor)

    def _apply(self, shape: Qt.CursorShape):
        """Применить курсор. На macOS — через NSCursor (глобально),
        плюс QApplication.setOverrideCursor как fallback."""
        if self._current_shape == shape:
            if NSCURSOR_AVAILABLE:
                self._apply_ns_cursor(shape, force=True)
            return

        if NSCURSOR_AVAILABLE:
            self._apply_ns_cursor(shape, force=True)
        else:
            if self._current_shape is None:
                QApplication.setOverrideCursor(shape)
            else:
                QApplication.changeOverrideCursor(shape)
        self._current_shape = shape

    def _apply_ns_cursor(self, shape: Qt.CursorShape, force: bool = False):
        name = _NS_CURSOR_MAP.get(shape)
        if not name:
            return
        if not force and self._current_ns_name == name:
            return
        try:
            getattr(NSCursor, name)().set()
            self._current_ns_name = name
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"NSCursor.{name}().set() упал: {e}")

# src/pik2video/gui/common/dialogs.py
======================================================================

# src/pik2video/gui/common/dialogs.py


from PySide6.QtWidgets import QMessageBox


def confirm(
    parent,
    text: str,
    title: str = "Подтверждение",
    translator=None,
    yes_label: str = "",
    no_label: str = "",
) -> bool:
    """
    Универсальное окно подтверждения.
    Возвращает True если пользователь нажал "Да".

    yes_label / no_label — переопределяют текст кнопок.
    Если не заданы, берётся из translator или дефолт.
    """
    msg = QMessageBox(parent)
    msg.setIcon(QMessageBox.Question)
    msg.setText(text)

    if translator:
        msg.setWindowTitle(translator.tr("confirm_title"))
        default_yes = translator.tr("confirm_yes")
        default_no = translator.tr("confirm_no")
    else:
        msg.setWindowTitle(title)
        default_yes = "Да"
        default_no = "Нет"

    btn_yes = msg.addButton(yes_label or default_yes, QMessageBox.YesRole)
    btn_no = msg.addButton(no_label or default_no, QMessageBox.NoRole)

    msg.setDefaultButton(btn_no)
    msg.exec()

    return msg.clickedButton() == btn_yes

def info(parent, text: str, title: str = "Информация", translator=None):
    """
    Информационное сообщение.
    """
    msg = QMessageBox(parent)
    
    if translator:
        msg.setWindowTitle(translator.tr("info_title"))
        msg.setText(text)
        msg.setIcon(QMessageBox.Information)
        msg.addButton(translator.tr("info_ok"), QMessageBox.AcceptRole)
    else:
        msg.setWindowTitle(title)
        msg.setText(text)
        msg.setIcon(QMessageBox.Information)
        msg.addButton("OK", QMessageBox.AcceptRole)
    
    msg.exec()


def warning(parent, text: str, title: str = "Предупреждение", translator=None):
    """
    Предупреждение.
    """
    msg = QMessageBox(parent)
    
    if translator:
        msg.setWindowTitle(translator.tr("warning_title"))
        msg.setText(text)
        msg.setIcon(QMessageBox.Warning)
        msg.addButton(translator.tr("warning_ok"), QMessageBox.AcceptRole)
    else:
        msg.setWindowTitle(title)
        msg.setText(text)
        msg.setIcon(QMessageBox.Warning)
        msg.addButton("OK", QMessageBox.AcceptRole)
    
    msg.exec()


def error(parent, text: str, title: str = "Ошибка", translator=None):
    """
    Сообщение об ошибке.
    """
    msg = QMessageBox(parent)
    
    if translator:
        msg.setWindowTitle(translator.tr("error_title"))
        msg.setText(text)
        msg.setIcon(QMessageBox.Critical)
        msg.addButton(translator.tr("error_ok"), QMessageBox.AcceptRole)
    else:
        msg.setWindowTitle(title)
        msg.setText(text)
        msg.setIcon(QMessageBox.Critical)
        msg.addButton("OK", QMessageBox.AcceptRole)
    
    msg.exec()

# src/pik2video/gui/common/tooltip.py

from PySide6.QtCore import QPoint, Qt, QTimer
from PySide6.QtGui import QFont
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QLabel, QPushButton

_tooltips_enabled = True

def set_tooltips_enabled(enabled: bool):
    """Включить или выключить показ всех подсказок."""
    global _tooltips_enabled
    _tooltips_enabled = bool(enabled)


def is_tooltips_enabled() -> bool:
    """Текущее состояние флага подсказок."""
    return _tooltips_enabled


class CustomToolTip(QLabel):
    """
    Кастомный тултип, который не исчезает при изменении фокуса или нажатии клавиш.
    """
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(None, Qt.ToolTip | Qt.FramelessWindowHint)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WA_ShowWithoutActivating)
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel {
                background-color: #2a2a2a;
                color: #e0e0e0;
                border: 1px solid #555;
                padding: 4px 8px;
                border-radius: 4px;
                font-size: 12px;
            }
        """)
        self.setFont(QFont("Arial", 10))
        self.hide()
        self._hide_timer = QTimer(self)
        self._hide_timer.setSingleShot(True)
        self._hide_timer.timeout.connect(self.hide)

    def show_text(self, text: str, global_pos: QPoint, duration_ms: int = 3000):
        """Показать тултип в указанной глобальной позиции."""
        self.setText(text)
        self.adjustSize()
        pos = global_pos + QPoint(15, 15)
        self.move(pos)
        self.show()
        self._hide_timer.start(duration_ms)

    def hide_tip(self):
        """Скрыть подсказку и остановить таймер."""
        self._hide_timer.stop()
        self.hide()




class TooltipButton(QPushButton):
    """Кнопка, которая показывает CustomToolTip при наведении."""

    def __init__(self, text: str = "", tooltip_text: str = "", parent=None):
        super().__init__(text, parent)
        self._tooltip_text = tooltip_text
        self._tooltip = None

    def set_tooltip(self, text: str):
        """Обновить текст подсказки (аналог setToolTip)."""
        self._tooltip_text = text

    def enterEvent(self, event):
        """Курсор навёлся на кнопку."""
        super().enterEvent(event)

        if not _tooltips_enabled:
            return

        if not self._tooltip_text:
            return



        if self._tooltip is None:
            self._tooltip = CustomToolTip()

        global_pos = self.mapToGlobal(self.rect().bottomLeft())

        self._tooltip.show_text(self._tooltip_text, global_pos)

    def leaveEvent(self, event):
        """Курсор ушёл с кнопки."""
        super().leaveEvent(event)
        if self._tooltip:
            self._tooltip.hide_tip()

# src/pik2video/gui/common/utils.py

# src/pik2video/gui/common/utils.py

from PySide6.QtCore import QPoint, QRect


def bottom_right_position(window, screen_geometry: QRect, margin: int = 0) -> QPoint:
    x = screen_geometry.right() - window.width() - margin
    y = screen_geometry.bottom() - window.height() - margin
    return QPoint(x, y)

def center_relative_to(parent_geometry: QRect, window) -> QPoint:
    x = parent_geometry.x() + (parent_geometry.width() - window.width()) // 2
    y = parent_geometry.y() + (parent_geometry.height() - window.height()) // 2
    return QPoint(x, y)


def center_to_parent(window, parent) -> QPoint:
    parent_geom = parent.frameGeometry()
    return center_relative_to(parent_geom, window)


def fullscreen_geometry(screen) -> QRect:
    return screen.geometry()


def bring_window_to_front(window):
    #window.show()
    window.raise_()
    window.activateWindow()

def keep_window_inside_screen(window, margin=120):
    """
    Перемещает окно внутрь видимого экрана, если оно выходит за границы.
    margin — отступ от краёв в пикселях.
    """
    screen = window.screen().availableGeometry()
    x = max(screen.left() + margin, min(window.x(), screen.right() - window.width() - margin))
    y = max(screen.top() + margin, min(window.y(), screen.bottom() - window.height() - margin))
    window.move(x, y)

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/crop_mode.py

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/crop_mode.py
"""
Контроллер режима настройки кадра (пресеты).

Отвечает за:
- переключение видимости панели пресетов в TopBar
- уведомление подписчиков о входе/выходе из режима

Кнопка режима — в TopBar (btn_crop). Не блокирует интерфейс:
пользователь может играть, ставить метки, работать с зонами и т.д.

Не знает:
- как применяется обрезка
- про EditorState
- про PlayerWidget и PresetFrame (это отдельный слой)
"""

import logging

from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, Signal

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class CropModeController(QObject):
    """Управляет режимом настройки кадра."""

    mode_opened = Signal()
    mode_closed = Signal()

    def __init__(self, editor_window, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._editor = editor_window
        self._active = False

    def is_active(self) -> bool:
        return self._active

    def toggle(self, checked: bool = None):
        """Переключить режим. `checked` приходит от checkable-кнопки TopBar."""
        if self._active:
            self.close()
        else:
            self.open()

    def open(self):
        if self._active:
            return
        if not self._editor.state.has_videos():
            logger.debug("Пресеты: нет видео — режим не включается")
            if hasattr(self._editor.top_bar, "btn_crop"):
                self._editor.top_bar.btn_crop.blockSignals(True)
                self._editor.top_bar.btn_crop.setChecked(False)
                self._editor.top_bar.btn_crop.blockSignals(False)
            return

        self._active = True
        self._editor.top_bar.show_presets(True)
        logger.debug("Режим пресетов открыт")
        self.mode_opened.emit()

    def close(self):
        if not self._active:
            return
        self._active = False
        self._editor.top_bar.show_presets(False)
        if hasattr(self._editor.top_bar, "btn_crop"):
            self._editor.top_bar.btn_crop.blockSignals(True)
            self._editor.top_bar.btn_crop.setChecked(False)
            self._editor.top_bar.btn_crop.blockSignals(False)
        logger.debug("Режим пресетов закрыт")
        self.mode_closed.emit()

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/export/__init__.py
"""
Пакет экспорта редактора.

Содержит:
- ExportController — управление экспортом
- ExportDialog — диалог прогресса
- ExportService — сборка команды ffmpeg и запуск процесса
"""

from .controller import ExportController
from .dialog import ExportDialog
from .service import ExportService

__all__ = ["ExportController", "ExportDialog", "ExportService"]


======================================================================
# src/pik2video/gui/editor/export/controller.py
======================================================================

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/export/controller.py
"""
Контроллер экспорта.

Отвечает за:
- запуск ExportService
- показ диалога прогресса
- обработку отмены
- показ результата (успех / ошибка)

Не знает:
- про layout редактора
- про другие зоны
"""

import logging

from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, Signal
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QMessageBox

from .dialog import ExportDialog
from .service import ExportService
from ..state import EditorState

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ExportController(QObject):
    """Управляет процессом экспорта."""

    export_started = Signal()
    export_progress = Signal(int)
    export_finished = Signal(bool, str)

    def __init__(self, editor_window, state: EditorState, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._editor = editor_window
        self._state = state

        self._service = ExportService(self)
        self._service.export_started.connect(self._on_started)
        self._service.export_progress.connect(self._on_progress)
        self._service.export_finished.connect(self._on_finished)

        self._dialog = None


    def is_running(self) -> bool:
        return self._service.is_running()

    def start(self):
        """Запустить экспорт с показом прогресса."""
        if not self._state.has_any_media():
            QMessageBox.warning(self._editor, "Экспорт", "Нет активного видеофайла.")
            return

        if self._service.is_running():
            QMessageBox.information(self._editor, "Экспорт", "Экспорт уже идёт.")
            return

        self._editor.bottom_panel.set_export_enabled(False)
        self._editor.bottom_panel.set_export_text("Экспорт...")

        if self._dialog is not None:                              # если окно от прошлого экспорта осталось
            self._dialog.deleteLater()                            # помечаем на удаление
            self._dialog = None                                   # снимаем ссылку

        self._dialog = ExportDialog(self._editor)                 # создаём новое окно прогресса
        self._dialog.cancel_requested.connect(self._on_cancel)
        self._dialog.show()

        self._service.start_export(self._state)

    def cancel(self):
        """Прервать текущий экспорт."""
        if self._dialog is not None:
            self._dialog.set_cancelling()
        self._service.cancel()


    def _on_cancel(self):
        self.cancel()

    def _on_started(self):
        logger.info("Экспорт: старт")
        self.export_started.emit()

    def _on_progress(self, percent: int):
        if self._dialog is not None:
            self._dialog.set_progress(percent)
        self.export_progress.emit(percent)

    def _on_finished(self, success: bool, message: str):
        self._editor.bottom_panel.set_export_enabled(True)
        self._editor.bottom_panel.set_export_text("Экспорт")

        if self._dialog is None:
            return

        if success:
            self._dialog.show_success(message)
        else:
            self._dialog.show_error(message)

        self.export_finished.emit(success, message)

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/export/dialog.py

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/export/dialog.py
"""
Диалог прогресса экспорта.

Отвечает только за:
- показ прогресс-бара
- кнопку «Отмена»
- показ результата (успех / ошибка)
- кнопку «Показать в Finder» после успеха

Не знает:
- про FFmpeg
- про EditorState
- про ExportService
"""

import logging
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal
from PySide6.QtGui import QFont
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QDialog, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QProgressBar, QPushButton, QVBoxLayout

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ExportDialog(QDialog):
    """Модальное окно прогресса экспорта."""

    cancel_requested = Signal()

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)

        self.setModal(True)
        self.setWindowTitle("Экспорт")
        self.setFixedWidth(420)
        self.setMinimumHeight(180)

        self._output_path = None
        self._is_done = False

        self._setup_ui()

    def _setup_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setSpacing(12)
        layout.setContentsMargins(20, 20, 20, 20)

        self.title_label = QLabel("Экспорт видео")
        self.title_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        font = QFont()
        font.setPointSize(12)
        font.setBold(True)
        self.title_label.setFont(font)
        self.title_label.setStyleSheet("color: #e0e0e0;")
        layout.addWidget(self.title_label)

        self.status_label = QLabel("Обработка...")
        self.status_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.status_label.setWordWrap(True)
        self.status_label.setStyleSheet("color: #aaa; font-size: 11px;")
        layout.addWidget(self.status_label)

        self.progress_bar = QProgressBar()
        self.progress_bar.setRange(0, 100)
        self.progress_bar.setValue(0)
        self.progress_bar.setStyleSheet("""
            QProgressBar {
                border: 1px solid #444;
                border-radius: 4px;
                background: #2a2a2a;
                text-align: center;
                color: #e0e0e0;
                height: 20px;
            }
            QProgressBar::chunk {
                background: #3a7a3a;
                border-radius: 4px;
            }
        """)
        layout.addWidget(self.progress_bar)

        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_layout.addStretch()

        self.btn_cancel = QPushButton("Отмена")
        self.btn_cancel.setFixedWidth(110)
        self.btn_cancel.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background-color: #3a3a3a;
                border: 1px solid #555;
                border-radius: 4px;
                color: #e0e0e0;
                font-size: 12px;
                padding: 6px 12px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #4a4a4a; }
            QPushButton:pressed { background-color: #2a2a2a; }
        """)
        self.btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self._on_cancel_clicked)

        self.btn_reveal = QPushButton("Показать в Finder")
        self.btn_reveal.setFixedWidth(160)
        self.btn_reveal.setVisible(False)
        self.btn_reveal.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background-color: #2a5a7a;
                border: 1px solid #3a7a9a;
                border-radius: 4px;
                color: white;
                font-size: 12px;
                padding: 6px 12px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #3a7a9a; }
            QPushButton:pressed { background-color: #1a4a6a; }
        """)
        self.btn_reveal.clicked.connect(self._on_reveal_clicked)

        self.btn_close = QPushButton("Закрыть")
        self.btn_close.setFixedWidth(110)
        self.btn_close.setVisible(False)
        self.btn_close.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background-color: #3a3a3a;
                border: 1px solid #555;
                border-radius: 4px;
                color: #e0e0e0;
                font-size: 12px;
                padding: 6px 12px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #4a4a4a; }
            QPushButton:pressed { background-color: #2a2a2a; }
        """)
        self.btn_close.clicked.connect(self.accept)

        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_cancel)
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_reveal)
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_close)
        btn_layout.addStretch()

        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)


    def set_progress(self, percent: int):
        self.progress_bar.setValue(percent)

    def is_done(self) -> bool:
        """Экспорт завершён (успех или ошибка)."""
        return self._is_done

    def show_success(self, output_path: str):
        self._is_done = True
        self._output_path = output_path

        self.title_label.setText("Экспорт завершён")
        self.title_label.setStyleSheet("color: #5fbf5f;")
        self.status_label.setText(Path(output_path).name)
        self.status_label.setStyleSheet("color: #aaa; font-size: 11px;")
        self.progress_bar.setValue(100)

        self.btn_cancel.setVisible(False)
        self.btn_reveal.setVisible(True)
        self.btn_close.setVisible(True)

    def show_error(self, message: str):
        self._is_done = True

        self.title_label.setText("Ошибка экспорта")
        self.title_label.setStyleSheet("color: #ff6b6b;")
        self.status_label.setText(message)
        self.status_label.setStyleSheet("color: #d0a0a0; font-size: 11px;")

        self.btn_cancel.setVisible(False)
        self.btn_close.setVisible(True)

    def set_cancelling(self):
        self.status_label.setText("Отмена...")
        self.btn_cancel.setEnabled(False)


    def _on_cancel_clicked(self):
        if self._is_done:
            return
        self.cancel_requested.emit()

    def _on_reveal_clicked(self):
        if not self._output_path:
            return
        path = Path(self._output_path)
        try:
            subprocess.run(["open", "-R", str(path)], check=False)   # показать файл в Finder
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"Не удалось открыть Finder: {e}")

        self.accept()                                                # закрыть диалог

    def closeEvent(self, event):
        if not self._is_done:
            self.cancel_requested.emit()
        super().closeEvent(event)


======================================================================
# src/pik2video/gui/editor/export/service.py
======================================================================

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/export/service.py
"""
Сборка и запуск команды FFmpeg.

Отвечает только за:
- построение списка аргументов
- вычисление выходного пути
- запуск FFmpeg через QProcess
- парсинг прогресса из stderr
- сигналы: started / progress / finished

Не знает:
- про виджеты
- про EditorState (принимает параметры через build_command)
"""

import logging
import math
import shutil
import subprocess
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, QProcess, Signal

from ..state import EditorState

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


FFMPEG_BIN = shutil.which("ffmpeg") or "/opt/homebrew/bin/ffmpeg"
FFPROBE_BIN = shutil.which("ffprobe") or "/opt/homebrew/bin/ffprobe"

FORMAT_EXTENSIONS = {
    "MP4": ".mp4",
    "MKV": ".mkv",
    "AVI": ".avi",
    "MOV": ".mov",
    "GIF": ".gif",
}

AUDIO_FORMAT_EXTENSIONS = {                                        # расширения для аудио-экспорта
    "WAV": ".wav",
    "MP3": ".mp3",
    "M4A": ".m4a",
}

RESOLUTION_HEIGHT = {
    "original": None,
    "1080p": 1080,
    "720p": 720,
    "480p": 480,
}

ROTATION_FILTER = {
    0: None,
    90: "transpose=1",
    180: "transpose=1,transpose=1",
    270: "transpose=2",
}


class ExportService(QObject):
    """Собирает команду и запускает FFmpeg."""

    export_started = Signal()
    export_progress = Signal(int)          # 0..100
    export_finished = Signal(bool, str)    # успех, сообщение

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._process = None
        self._duration = 0.0
        self._output_path = None


    def start_export(self, state: EditorState):
        """Собрать команду и запустить FFmpeg."""
        if self._process is not None:
            logger.warning("Экспорт уже запущен")
            return

        try:
            args = self.build_command(state)
            output = self.get_output_path(state)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Ошибка сборки команды: {e}")
            self.export_finished.emit(False, str(e))
            return

        output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        free_space = shutil.disk_usage(output.parent).free                 # свободное место в байтах
        source_path = state.get_video_path() or state.get_audio_path()     # путь к исходнику
        source_size = Path(source_path).stat().st_size if source_path else 0
        required = source_size * 2                                          # запас ×2

        if free_space < required:
            free_mb = free_space // (1024 * 1024)
            need_mb = required // (1024 * 1024)
            msg = f"Мало места на диске: свободно {free_mb} МБ, нужно около {need_mb} МБ."
            logger.error(msg)
            self.export_finished.emit(False, msg)
            return

        self._output_path = output
        self._duration = self._get_export_duration(state)

        logger.info(f"Запуск FFmpeg: {' '.join(args)}")

        self._process = QProcess(self)
        self._process.setProcessChannelMode(QProcess.MergedChannels)
        self._process.readyReadStandardOutput.connect(self._on_output)
        self._process.finished.connect(self._on_finished)
        self._process.errorOccurred.connect(self._on_error)

        self.export_started.emit()
        self._process.start(args[0], args[1:])

    def cancel(self):
        """Прервать процесс."""
        if self._process is not None:
            logger.info("Экспорт отменён пользователем")
            self._process.kill()
            self._process.waitForFinished(2000)
            self._process.deleteLater()
            self._process = None

    def is_running(self) -> bool:
        return self._process is not None


    def build_command(self, state: EditorState) -> list:
        """Точка входа. Выбирает ветку: видео или аудио."""
        if state.has_videos():
            if state.has_audio():
                return self._build_video_with_audio_command(state)   # видео + отдельный аудиофайл
            return self._build_video_command(state)                  # только видео
        if state.has_audio():
            return self._build_audio_command(state)                  # только аудио
        raise ValueError("Нет медиа для экспорта")

    def _video_has_audio(self, video_path: str) -> bool:
        """Проверить, есть ли звуковая дорожка в видеофайле."""
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(
                [
                    FFPROBE_BIN, "-v", "error",
                    "-select_streams", "a",
                    "-show_entries", "stream=index",
                    "-of", "csv=p=0",
                    video_path,
                ],
                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5,
            )
            return bool(result.stdout.strip())
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"Не удалось проверить аудио-дорожку в видео: {e}")
            return False

    def _build_video_with_audio_command(self, state: EditorState) -> list:
        """Собрать команду для видео + отдельный аудиофайл (микширование)."""
        video_path = state.get_video_path()
        audio_path = state.get_audio_path()
        if not video_path or not audio_path:
            raise ValueError("Нет видео или аудио")

        has_video_audio = self._video_has_audio(video_path)

        args = [FFMPEG_BIN, "-y"]

        v_start, v_end = state.get_trim()
        v_duration = state.get_video_duration()
        if v_start > 0:
            args += ["-ss", f"{v_start:.3f}"]
        if v_end > 0 and (v_duration <= 0 or v_end < v_duration):
            args += ["-to", f"{v_end:.3f}"]
        args += ["-i", video_path]

        a_start, a_end = state.get_audio_trim()
        a_duration = state.get_audio_duration()
        audio_offset = state.get_audio_offset()

        shift_in_output = (audio_offset + a_start) - v_start
        if shift_in_output >= 0:
            a_start_input = a_start
            adelay_ms = int(shift_in_output * 1000)
        else:
            a_start_input = max(0.0, a_start - shift_in_output)   # пропустить лишнее начало
            adelay_ms = 0
        logger.info(f"[EXPORT] audio_offset={audio_offset:.3f}, v_start={v_start:.3f}, "
                    f"a_start={a_start:.3f}, a_start_input={a_start_input:.3f}, adelay={adelay_ms}ms")

        if a_start_input > 0:
            args += ["-ss", f"{a_start_input:.3f}"]
        if a_end > 0 and (a_duration <= 0 or a_end < a_duration):
            args += ["-to", f"{a_end:.3f}"]
        args += ["-i", audio_path]

        video_volume = state.get_video_volume()
        audio_volume = state.get_audio_volume()
        speed = state.get_speed()

        filter_parts = []

        has_zoom_markers = any(z != 0 for _, _, z in state.get_markers())

        if has_zoom_markers:
            seg_filter, seg_out = self._build_segmented_video_chain(state)
            filter_parts.append(seg_filter)

            post = []
            rot = ROTATION_FILTER.get(state.get_rotation())
            if rot:
                post.append(rot)
            h = RESOLUTION_HEIGHT.get(state.get_resolution())
            if h:
                post.append(f"scale=-2:{h}")
            if speed != 1.0:
                post.append(f"setpts=PTS/{speed}")

            if post:
                filter_parts.append(f"{seg_out}{','.join(post)}[vout]")
            else:
                filter_parts.append(f"{seg_out}null[vout]")
        else:
            v_filters = []
            crop = self._build_crop_filter(state)
            if crop:
                v_filters.append(crop)
            rot = ROTATION_FILTER.get(state.get_rotation())
            if rot:
                v_filters.append(rot)
            h = RESOLUTION_HEIGHT.get(state.get_resolution())
            if h:
                v_filters.append(f"scale=-2:{h}")
            if speed != 1.0:
                v_filters.append(f"setpts=PTS/{speed}")

            if v_filters:
                filter_parts.append(f"[0:v]{','.join(v_filters)}[vout]")
            else:
                filter_parts.append("[0:v]null[vout]")

        a_filters = []
        if adelay_ms > 0:
            a_filters.append(f"adelay={adelay_ms}:all=1")           # сдвиг аудио вправо (мс)
        if speed != 1.0:
            a_filters.append(self._build_atempo(speed))
        a_filters.append(f"volume={audio_volume:.3f}")
        filter_parts.append(f"[1:a]{','.join(a_filters)}[aa]")

        if has_video_audio:
            va_filters = []
            if speed != 1.0:
                va_filters.append(self._build_atempo(speed))
            va_filters.append(f"volume={video_volume:.3f}")
            filter_parts.append(f"[0:a]{','.join(va_filters)}[va]")
            filter_parts.append("[va][aa]amix=inputs=2:duration=first:normalize=0[aout]")
        else:
            filter_parts.append("[aa]anull[aout]")

        filter_complex = ";".join(filter_parts)
        args += ["-filter_complex", filter_complex]
        args += ["-map", "[vout]", "-map", "[aout]"]

        if not has_video_audio:
            args += ["-shortest"]

        if state.get_format() != "GIF":
            args += ["-c:v", "libx264"]
            if state.get_bitrate_mode() == "manual":
                args += ["-b:v", state.get_bitrate_value()]
            else:
                args += ["-crf", "23"]
            args += ["-pix_fmt", "yuv420p"]
            args += ["-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "128k"]

        args.append(str(self.get_output_path(state)))
        return args


    def _build_video_command(self, state: EditorState) -> list:
        input_path = state.get_video_path()
        if not input_path:
            raise ValueError("Нет активного видеофайла")

        args = [FFMPEG_BIN, "-y"]

        start, end = state.get_trim()
        duration = state.get_video_duration()
        if start > 0:
            args += ["-ss", f"{start:.3f}"]
        if end > 0 and (duration <= 0 or end < duration):
            args += ["-to", f"{end:.3f}"]
        args += ["-i", input_path]


        has_zoom_markers = any(z != 0 for _, _, z in state.get_markers())
        speed = state.get_speed()

        if has_zoom_markers:
            filter_complex, vout_label = self._build_segmented_video_chain(state)

            post = []
            rot = ROTATION_FILTER.get(state.get_rotation())
            if rot:
                post.append(rot)
            height = RESOLUTION_HEIGHT.get(state.get_resolution())
            if height:
                post.append(f"scale=-2:{height}")
            if speed != 1.0:
                post.append(f"setpts=PTS/{speed}")

            if post:
                filter_complex += f";{vout_label}{','.join(post)}[vfinal]"
                vout_label = "[vfinal]"

            args += ["-filter_complex", filter_complex]
            args += ["-map", vout_label, "-map", "0:a?"]
        else:
            filters = []

            crop_filter = self._build_crop_filter(state)
            if crop_filter:
                filters.append(crop_filter)

            rot_filter = ROTATION_FILTER.get(state.get_rotation())
            if rot_filter:
                filters.append(rot_filter)

            height = RESOLUTION_HEIGHT.get(state.get_resolution())
            if height:
                filters.append(f"scale=-2:{height}")

            if speed != 1.0:
                filters.append(f"setpts=PTS/{speed}")

            if filters:
                args += ["-vf", ",".join(filters)]

        if state.get_format() != "GIF":
            args += ["-c:v", "libx264"]
            if state.get_bitrate_mode() == "manual":
                args += ["-b:v", state.get_bitrate_value()]
            else:
                args += ["-crf", "23"]
            args += ["-pix_fmt", "yuv420p"]

        if state.get_format() != "GIF":
            args += ["-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "128k"]
            if speed != 1.0:
                args += ["-af", self._build_atempo(speed)]

        args.append(str(self.get_output_path(state)))
        return args

    def _build_audio_command(self, state: EditorState) -> list:
        """Собрать команду FFmpeg для экспорта аудио-онли."""
        input_path = state.get_audio_path()
        if not input_path:
            raise ValueError("Нет активного аудиофайла")

        args = [FFMPEG_BIN, "-y", "-i", input_path]

        start, end = state.get_audio_trim()
        duration = state.get_audio_duration()
        if start > 0:
            args += ["-ss", f"{start:.3f}"]                        # обрезка начала
        if end > 0 and (duration <= 0 or end < duration):
            args += ["-to", f"{end:.3f}"]                          # обрезка конца

        speed = state.get_speed()
        if speed != 1.0:
            args += ["-af", self._build_atempo(speed)]             # скорость аудио-онли

        fmt = state.get_audio_format()
        if fmt == "WAV":
            args += ["-c:a", "pcm_s16le"]                          # без потерь
        elif fmt == "MP3":
            args += ["-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-q:a", "2"]            # VBR ~190 kbps
        elif fmt == "M4A":
            args += ["-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k"]                # AAC 192 kbps
        else:
            args += ["-c:a", "pcm_s16le"]                          # fallback на WAV

        args.append(str(self.get_output_path(state)))
        return args

    def get_output_path(self, state: EditorState) -> Path:
        base = state.get_export_path()
        if not base:
            base = str(Path.home() / "Desktop")
        base_dir = Path(base)

        if state.has_videos():
            name = state.get_export_filename()
            if not name:
                name = datetime.now().strftime("output_%m.%d_%H.%M.%S")
            ext = FORMAT_EXTENSIONS.get(state.get_format(), ".mp4")
        elif state.has_audio():
            name = state.get_audio_export_filename()
            if not name:
                name = datetime.now().strftime("output_%Y.%m.%d_%H.%M.%S")
            ext = AUDIO_FORMAT_EXTENSIONS.get(state.get_audio_format(), ".wav")
        else:
            name = "output"
            ext = ".mp4"

        final = base_dir / f"{name}{ext}"
        if final.exists():
            counter = 1
            while True:
                candidate = base_dir / f"{name}_{counter}{ext}"
                if not candidate.exists():
                    final = candidate
                    break
                counter += 1
        return final

    def _get_export_duration(self, state: EditorState) -> float:
        """Длительность экспорта с учётом типа медиа, трима и скорости."""
        if state.has_audio() and not state.has_videos():
            start, end = state.get_audio_trim()
            duration = state.get_audio_duration()
            if duration <= 0:
                return 0.0
            end = end if end > 0 else duration
            return max(0.0, end - start)

        start, end = state.get_trim()
        duration = state.get_video_duration()
        if duration <= 0:
            return 0.0
        end = end if end > 0 else duration
        length = max(0.0, end - start)
        speed = state.get_speed()
        if speed > 0:
            length /= speed
        return length


    def _on_output(self):
        if self._process is None:
            return

        data = self._process.readAllStandardOutput().data().decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")

        for line in data.splitlines():
            if "time=" not in line:
                continue
            try:
                time_part = line.split("time=")[1].split()[0]
                h, m, s = time_part.split(":")
                seconds = int(h) * 3600 + int(m) * 60 + float(s)
                if self._duration > 0:
                    percent = min(100, int(seconds / self._duration * 100))
                    self.export_progress.emit(percent)
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _on_finished(self, exit_code: int, _exit_status):
        success = exit_code == 0
        output = str(self._output_path) if self._output_path else ""

        if success:
            logger.info(f"Экспорт завершён: {output}")
            self.export_finished.emit(True, output)
        else:
            logger.error(f"FFmpeg завершился с кодом {exit_code}")
            self.export_finished.emit(False, f"FFmpeg завершился с кодом {exit_code}")

        self._process.deleteLater()
        self._process = None
        self._output_path = None

    def _on_error(self, error):
        logger.error(f"Ошибка QProcess: {error}")
        self.export_finished.emit(False, f"Ошибка процесса: {error}")
        self._process.deleteLater()
        self._process = None
        self._output_path = None


    def _build_atempo(self, speed: float) -> str:
        if 0.5 <= speed <= 2.0:
            return f"atempo={speed}"

        if speed < 0.5:
            parts = []
            remaining = speed
            while remaining < 0.5:
                parts.append("atempo=0.5")
                remaining /= 0.5
            parts.append(f"atempo={remaining}")
            return ",".join(parts)

        parts = []
        remaining = speed
        while remaining > 2.0:
            parts.append("atempo=2.0")
            remaining /= 2.0
        parts.append(f"atempo={remaining}")
        return ",".join(parts)


    def _build_x_expr(self, cx_base: float, sw: int, W: int, markers: list[tuple[float, float]]) -> str:
        """Построить выражение для x в фильтре crop в зависимости от времени."""
        base = int(round(cx_base))
        max_x = max(0, sw - W)
        base = max(0, min(base, max_x))
        
        offsets = []
        for t, off in markers:
            x = base + int(round(off))
            x = max(0, min(x, max_x))
            offsets.append((t, x))
        
        EPS = 0.0005

        expr = str(offsets[-1][1])
        for i in range(len(offsets) - 2, -1, -1):
            t_next = max(0.0, offsets[i+1][0] - EPS)
            expr_i = str(offsets[i][1])
            expr = f"if(lt(t,{t_next:.4f}),{expr_i},{expr})"

        t0 = max(0.0, offsets[0][0] - EPS)
        expr = f"if(lt(t,{t0:.4f}),{base},{expr})"

        return expr

    PRESET_CANVAS = {
        "16:9": (1920, 1080),
        "9:16": (1080, 1920),
        "1:1":  (1080, 1080),
        "4:5":  (1080, 1350),
        "4:3":  (1600, 1200),
    }

    def _get_canvas_size(self, state: EditorState) -> tuple:
        """Размер холста экспорта: фиксированный по пресету, для original — исходник."""
        preset = state.get_aspect_preset()
        if preset == "original" or preset not in self.PRESET_CANVAS:
            sw, sh = state.get_source_resolution()
            if sw <= 0 or sh <= 0:
                return (1920, 1080)
            return (int(sw) & ~1, int(sh) & ~1)
        return self.PRESET_CANVAS[preset]

    def _build_segmented_video_chain(self, state: EditorState) -> tuple:
        """filter_complex-цепочка для видео с сегментацией по меткам.

        Возвращает (строка_фильтров, out_label). out_label — метка потока с результатом
        (например "[vconcat]" или "[v0]"), к которой дописываются общие фильтры
        (rotation, resolution, speed) в вызывающем коде.
        """
        trim_start, trim_end = state.get_trim()
        duration = state.get_video_duration()
        if trim_end <= 0 or (duration > 0 and trim_end > duration):
            trim_end = duration if duration > 0 else trim_end
        total_dur = trim_end - trim_start
        if total_dur <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Нулевая длительность — нечего экспортировать")

        raw_markers = state.get_markers()
        markers = []
        for t, off, z, _p in raw_markers:
            t_norm = t - trim_start
            if 0.001 < t_norm < total_dur - 0.001:
                markers.append((t_norm, off, z))
        markers.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])

        segments = []
        if not markers:
            segments.append((0.0, total_dur, 0.0, 0))
        else:
            if markers[0][0] > 0.001:
                segments.append((0.0, markers[0][0], 0.0, 0))
            for i, (t, off, z) in enumerate(markers):
                seg_end = markers[i + 1][0] if i + 1 < len(markers) else total_dur
                segments.append((t, seg_end, off, z))

        CW, CH = self._get_canvas_size(state)
        sw, sh = state.get_source_resolution()

        parts = []
        labels = []
        for i, (seg_start, seg_end, off, zoom) in enumerate(segments):
            cx, cy, cw, ch = state.compute_crop_for(zoom, off)
            if cw <= 0 or ch <= 0:
                continue
            scale_ratio = CW / cw

            vwx = max(0, cx)
            vwy = max(0, cy)
            vwx_end = min(sw, cx + cw)
            vwy_end = min(sh, cy + ch)
            vww = max(1, int(round(vwx_end - vwx)))
            vwh = max(1, int(round(vwy_end - vwy)))

            scale_w = max(2, int(round(vww * scale_ratio))) & ~1
            scale_h = max(2, int(round(vwh * scale_ratio))) & ~1
            pad_x = max(0, int(round(max(0.0, -cx) * scale_ratio))) & ~1
            pad_y = max(0, int(round(max(0.0, -cy) * scale_ratio))) & ~1

            if scale_w + pad_x > CW:
                scale_w = CW - pad_x
            if scale_h + pad_y > CH:
                scale_h = CH - pad_y
            scale_w &= ~1
            scale_h &= ~1

            trim_s = math.floor(seg_start * 1000) / 1000
            trim_e = math.floor(seg_end * 1000) / 1000
            parts.append(
                f"[0:v]trim=start={trim_s:.3f}:end={trim_e:.3f},"
                f"setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
                f"crop={vww}:{vwh}:{int(round(vwx))}:{int(round(vwy))},"
                f"scale={scale_w}:{scale_h}:flags=lanczos,"
                f"pad={CW}:{CH}:{pad_x}:{pad_y}:color=black,"
                f"setsar=1[v{i}]"
            )
            labels.append(f"[v{i}]")

        if not labels:
            raise ValueError("Не удалось построить ни одного сегмента")

        if len(labels) == 1:
            return (";".join(parts), labels[0])

        parts.append(f"{''.join(labels)}concat=n={len(labels)}:v=1:a=0[vconcat]")
        return (";".join(parts), "[vconcat]")

    def _build_crop_filter(self, state: EditorState) -> str:
        """Собрать crop/pad по рабочей зоне _crop_rect (координаты источника)."""
        cx, cy, cw, ch = state.get_crop_rect()
        sw, sh = state.get_source_resolution()

        if sw <= 0 or sh <= 0 or cw <= 0 or ch <= 0:
            return ""

        markers = state.get_markers()
        markers_offsets = [(m[0], m[1]) for m in markers] if markers else []
        if markers_offsets and cw < sw:
            vx0 = max(0.0, cx)
            vy0 = max(0.0, cy)
            vx1 = min(float(sw), cx + cw)
            vy1 = min(float(sh), cy + ch)

            W = int(vx1 - vx0) & ~1   # видимая ширина
            H = int(vy1 - vy0) & ~1   # видимая высота
            Y = int(vy0) & ~1         # Y видимой части (не cy!)

            if W <= 0 or H <= 0:
                return ""

            cx_base = cx - state.get_offset_x()
            X_expr = self._build_x_expr(cx_base, sw, W, markers_offsets)
            X_expr_safe = X_expr.replace(",", "\\,")

            crop_part = f"crop={W}:{H}:{X_expr_safe}:{Y}"

            pad_w = int(cw) & ~1
            pad_h = int(ch) & ~1
            if pad_w > W or pad_h > H:
                pad_x = max(0, int(round(-cx)))
                pad_y = max(0, int(round(-cy)))
                pad_part = f"pad={pad_w}:{pad_h}:{pad_x}:{pad_y}:color=black"
                return f"{crop_part},{pad_part}"

            return crop_part

        EPS = 0.5
        if (abs(cx) < EPS and abs(cy) < EPS
                and abs(cw - sw) < EPS and abs(ch - sh) < EPS):
            return ""

        vx0 = max(0.0, cx)
        vy0 = max(0.0, cy)
        vx1 = min(float(sw), cx + cw)
        vy1 = min(float(sh), cy + ch)
        if vx1 <= vx0 or vy1 <= vy0:
            return ""

        crw = vx1 - vx0
        crh = vy1 - vy0
        W = int(crw) & ~1
        H = int(crh) & ~1
        X = int(vx0) & ~1
        Y = int(vy0) & ~1
        if W <= 0 or H <= 0:
            return ""

        if W == sw and H == sh and X == 0 and Y == 0:
            crop_part = ""
        else:
            crop_part = f"crop={W}:{H}:{X}:{Y}"

        pad_w = int(cw) & ~1
        pad_h = int(ch) & ~1
        if pad_w > W or pad_h > H:
            pad_x = max(0, int(round(vx0 - cx)))
            pad_y = max(0, int(round(vy0 - cy)))
            pad_part = f"pad={pad_w}:{pad_h}:{pad_x}:{pad_y}:color=black"
            if crop_part:
                return f"{crop_part},{pad_part}"
            return pad_part

        return crop_part

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/bottom_panel.py
"""
Нижняя панель редактора (зона 3).

Содержит:
- поле имени файла
- путь экспорта (кнопка)
- кнопку «Экспорт»
- кнопку «Очистить» в dashed-рамке

Публикует сигналы наружу.
"""

import logging

from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QFrame, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QLineEdit, QPushButton, QVBoxLayout, QWidget

from ...common.styles import BOTTOM_PANEL_QSS

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

DROP_AREA_WIDTH = 108
BOTTOM_PANEL_HEIGHT = 44
EXPORT_NAME_WIDTH = 220


class BottomPanel(QWidget):
    """Нижняя панель: экспорт + очистка."""

    export_clicked = Signal()
    clear_clicked = Signal()
    export_path_clicked = Signal()
    export_name_changed = Signal(str)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self):
        self.setFixedHeight(BOTTOM_PANEL_HEIGHT)
        self.setObjectName("bottomPanel")
        self.setStyleSheet(BOTTOM_PANEL_QSS)

        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(6, 2, 0, 2)
        layout.setSpacing(8)

        self.export_block = QWidget()
        self.export_block.setObjectName("exportBlock")
        export_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.export_block)
        export_layout.setContentsMargins(8, 2, 4, 2)
        export_layout.setSpacing(6)

        self.export_name_label = QLabel("имя файла")
        self.export_name_label.setStyleSheet(
            "color: #888; font-size: 11px; "
            "background: transparent; border: none;"
        )

        self.export_name_frame = QFrame()
        self.export_name_frame.setObjectName("exportNameFrame")
        self.export_name_frame.setFixedWidth(EXPORT_NAME_WIDTH)
        name_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.export_name_frame)
        name_layout.setContentsMargins(6, 5, 6, 5)
        name_layout.setSpacing(0)

        self.export_name_input = QLineEdit()
        self.export_name_input.setFrame(False)
        self.export_name_input.setEnabled(False)
        self.export_name_input.setAlignment(Qt.AlignRight | Qt.AlignVCenter)
        self.export_name_input.setStyleSheet("""
            QLineEdit {
                background: transparent;
                border: none;
                color: #e0e0e0;
                font-size: 11px;
                padding: 0px;
            }
            QLineEdit:disabled {
                color: #555;
                background: transparent;
            }
        """)


        self.export_name_input.textChanged.connect(self.export_name_changed.emit)

        self.export_ext_label = QLabel("")
        self.export_ext_label.setStyleSheet(
            "color: #666; font-size: 11px; "
            "background: transparent; border: none;"
        )

        name_layout.addWidget(self.export_name_input, stretch=1)
        name_layout.addWidget(self.export_ext_label, stretch=0)

        self.export_path_value = QPushButton("")
        self.export_path_value.setObjectName("exportPathValue")
        self.export_path_value.setFlat(True)
        self.export_path_value.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        self.export_path_value.clicked.connect(self.export_path_clicked.emit)

        self.btn_export = QPushButton("Экспорт")
        self.btn_export.setObjectName("btnExport")
        self.btn_export.setFixedHeight(24)
        self.btn_export.setFixedWidth(100)
        self.btn_export.clicked.connect(self.export_clicked.emit)
        self.btn_export.setEnabled(False)

        export_layout.addWidget(self.export_name_frame, stretch=0)
        export_layout.addWidget(self.export_name_label, stretch=0)
        export_layout.addStretch(1)
        export_layout.addWidget(self.export_path_value, stretch=0)
        export_layout.addSpacing(12)
        export_layout.addWidget(self.btn_export, stretch=0)

        layout.addWidget(self.export_block, stretch=1)

        self.clear_frame = QFrame()
        self.clear_frame.setObjectName("clearFrame")
        self.clear_frame.setFixedWidth(DROP_AREA_WIDTH)
        clear_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.clear_frame)
        clear_layout.setContentsMargins(6, 6, 6, 6)
        clear_layout.setSpacing(0)

        self.btn_clear = QPushButton("Очистить")
        self.btn_clear.setObjectName("btnClear")
        self.btn_clear.setFixedHeight(24)
        self.btn_clear.clicked.connect(self.clear_clicked.emit)

        clear_layout.addStretch()
        clear_layout.addWidget(self.btn_clear)

        layout.addWidget(self.clear_frame, stretch=0)

        self.set_clear_enabled(False)
        self.set_export_path("")
        self.set_editing_enabled(False)


    def set_export_enabled(self, enabled: bool):
        self.btn_export.setEnabled(enabled)

    def set_export_text(self, text: str):
        self.btn_export.setText(text)

    def set_export_filename(self, stem: str, ext: str):
        if stem:
            self.export_name_input.setPlaceholderText("")
            self.export_name_input.blockSignals(True)
            self.export_name_input.setText(stem)
            self.export_name_input.blockSignals(False)
            self.export_ext_label.setText(ext)
        else:
            self.export_name_input.setPlaceholderText("output_m.d_hh.mm.ss")
            self.export_name_input.blockSignals(True)
            self.export_name_input.setText("")
            self.export_name_input.blockSignals(False)
            self.export_ext_label.setText("")

    def set_export_path(self, path: str):
        self.export_path_value.setText(path)

    def set_editing_enabled(self, enabled: bool):
        """Включить/выключить поля ввода: имя файла, путь экспорта."""
        self.export_name_input.setEnabled(enabled)
        self.export_path_value.setEnabled(enabled)

    def set_clear_enabled(self, enabled: bool):
        self.btn_clear.setEnabled(enabled)

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/panels/drop_area.py

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/drop_area.py
"""
Правая колонка редактора (зона 4).

Содержит:
- панель со списком видеофайлов (VideoFilesPanel)
- drop-зону для аудиофайлов (DropZone)

Публикует сигналы наружу.
"""

import logging

from PySide6.QtCore import Signal
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QVBoxLayout, QWidget

from .drop_zone import DropZone
from .link_button import LinkButton
from .video_files_panel import VideoFilesPanel

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

DROP_AREA_WIDTH = 108
DROP_VIDEO_RATIO = 1
DROP_AUDIO_RATIO = 1
GAP = 4

VIDEO_EXTENSIONS = {".mp4", ".mov", ".mkv", ".avi", ".m4v"}
AUDIO_EXTENSIONS = {".mp3", ".wav", ".m4a", ".aac", ".flac"}


class DropArea(QWidget):
    """Колонка из двух зон: видео (2/3) и аудио (1/3)."""

    video_dropped = Signal(str)
    video_selected = Signal(int)
    video_delete_requested = Signal()
    audio_dropped = Signal(str)
    audio_delete_requested = Signal()
    audio_clicked = Signal()                                # клик по аудио-зоне (переключение режима плеера)
    link_toggled = Signal(bool)                             # переключение режима связки

    def __init__(self, thumbnail_loader, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._thumbnail_loader = thumbnail_loader          # загрузчик миниатюр из EditorWindow
        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self):
        self.setFixedWidth(DROP_AREA_WIDTH)
        self.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; border: none;")

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(GAP)

        self.drop_video = VideoFilesPanel(
            extensions=VIDEO_EXTENSIONS,
            thumbnail_loader=self._thumbnail_loader,
        )
        self.drop_video.file_dropped.connect(self.video_dropped.emit)
        self.drop_video.file_selected.connect(self.video_selected.emit)
        self.drop_video.delete_requested.connect(self.video_delete_requested.emit)
        layout.addWidget(self.drop_video, stretch=DROP_VIDEO_RATIO)

        self.link_button = LinkButton(linked=True)
        self.link_button.state_toggled.connect(self.link_toggled.emit)
        layout.addWidget(self.link_button, stretch=0)

        self.drop_audio = DropZone(
            icon="🎵",
            label="mp3",
            extensions=AUDIO_EXTENSIONS,
            with_delete_button=True,
        )
        self.drop_audio.file_dropped.connect(self.audio_dropped.emit)
        self.drop_audio.delete_requested.connect(self.audio_delete_requested.emit)
        self.drop_audio.clicked.connect(self.audio_clicked.emit)
        layout.addWidget(self.drop_audio, stretch=DROP_AUDIO_RATIO)


    def set_video_files(self, files: list, active_index: int):
        self.drop_video.set_files(files, active_index)

    def set_video_active(self, active_index: int):
        self.drop_video.set_active(active_index)

    def show_audio(self, path: str):
        self.drop_audio.show_audio(path)

    def show_audio_empty(self):
        self.drop_audio.show_empty(icon="🎵", label="mp3")

    def set_linked(self, value: bool):
        """Обновить визуальное состояние кнопки связки."""
        self.link_button.set_linked(value)

    def set_link_enabled(self, enabled: bool):
        """Включить/выключить кнопку связки (нужны оба файла)."""
        self.link_button.setEnabled(enabled)

    def update_highlight(self, mode: str, linked: bool):
        """Подсветка зон по режиму и связке."""
        if linked:
            self.drop_video.set_active_zone(True)
            self.drop_audio.set_active(True)
            self.drop_video.set_dimmed(False)
            self.drop_audio.set_dimmed(False)
        else:
            video_active = (mode == "video")
            audio_active = (mode == "audio")
            self.drop_video.set_active_zone(video_active)
            self.drop_audio.set_active(audio_active)
            self.drop_video.set_dimmed(not video_active)
            self.drop_audio.set_dimmed(not audio_active)

    def set_audio_active(self, active: bool):
        """Подсветить/снять подсветку аудио-зоны (режим плеера audio)."""
        self.drop_audio.set_active(active)

    def set_video_zone_active(self, active: bool):
        """Подсветить/снять подсветку видео-зоны."""
        self.drop_video.set_active_zone(active)

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/panels/drop_zone.py
======================================================================

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/panels/drop_zone.py
import logging
from pathlib import Path

from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QFrame, QLabel, QVBoxLayout, QWidget

from .file_tile import FileTile

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

BG_NORMAL = "#2a2a2a"
BG_HOVER = "#3d3d3d"
BORDER_NORMAL = "#555"
BORDER_HOVER = "#7a7a7a"

class DropZone(QFrame):
    file_dropped = Signal(str)
    delete_requested = Signal()
    clicked = Signal()

    def __init__(self, icon: str, label: str, extensions: set,
                 with_delete_button: bool = False, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._extensions = extensions
        self._is_hovered = False
        self._has_file = False
        self._is_active = False
        self._is_dimmed = False
        self._file_tile = None

        self.setAcceptDrops(True)
        self.setFrameShape(QFrame.NoFrame)

        self._root_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self._root_layout.setContentsMargins(6, 6, 6, 6)
        self._root_layout.setSpacing(4)

        self._placeholder_widget = QWidget()
        placeholder_layout = QVBoxLayout(self._placeholder_widget)
        placeholder_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        placeholder_layout.setSpacing(4)
        placeholder_layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)

        self._icon_label = QLabel(icon)
        self._icon_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self._icon_label.setAttribute(Qt.WA_TransparentForMouseEvents)
        self._icon_label.setStyleSheet("font-size: 28px; background: transparent; border: none;")

        self._text_label = QLabel(label)
        self._text_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self._text_label.setAttribute(Qt.WA_TransparentForMouseEvents)
        self._text_label.setWordWrap(True)
        self._text_label.setStyleSheet("color: #888; font-size: 11px; background: transparent; border: none;")

        placeholder_layout.addWidget(self._icon_label)
        placeholder_layout.addWidget(self._text_label)

        self._root_layout.addWidget(self._placeholder_widget, stretch=0, alignment=Qt.AlignCenter)
        self._root_layout.addStretch(1)

        self._apply_style()

    def _apply_style(self):
        if not self._has_file:
            bg = BG_NORMAL
            border = BORDER_NORMAL
        elif self._is_active:
            bg = "#3a3a3a"
            border = "#8a6f2a"
        elif self._is_dimmed:
            bg = "#1a1a1a"
            border = "#2a2a2a"
        elif self._is_hovered:
            bg = BG_HOVER
            border = BORDER_HOVER
        else:
            bg = BG_NORMAL
            border = BORDER_NORMAL

        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            DropZone {{
                background-color: {bg};
                border: 2px dashed {border};
                border-radius: 6px;
            }}
        """)

    def _set_hovered(self, value: bool):
        if self._is_hovered != value:
            self._is_hovered = value
            self._apply_style()

    def set_active(self, active: bool):
        if self._is_active != active:
            self._is_active = active
            self._apply_style()
            if self._file_tile is not None:
                self._file_tile.set_active(active)

    def set_dimmed(self, dimmed: bool):
        if self._is_dimmed != dimmed:
            self._is_dimmed = dimmed
            self._apply_style()
            if self._file_tile is not None:
                self._file_tile.set_dimmed(dimmed)

    def show_audio(self, path: str):
        self._has_file = True
        self._placeholder_widget.hide()
        
        if self._file_tile is not None:
            self._file_tile.hide()
            self._file_tile.deleteLater()
            self._file_tile = None

        self._file_tile = FileTile(path, is_active=self._is_active)
        self._file_tile.set_dimmed(self._is_dimmed)
        self._file_tile.clicked.connect(self.clicked.emit)
        self._file_tile.deleted.connect(self.delete_requested.emit)
        
        self._root_layout.insertWidget(self._root_layout.count() - 1, self._file_tile)
        
        self._apply_style()

    def show_empty(self, icon: str, label: str):
        self._has_file = False
        if self._file_tile is not None:
            self._file_tile.hide()
            self._file_tile.deleteLater()
            self._file_tile = None
        self._placeholder_widget.show()
        self._icon_label.setText(icon)
        self._text_label.setText(label)
        self._apply_style()

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            self.clicked.emit()
        super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def _is_valid_path(self, path: str) -> bool:
        if not path:
            return False
        return Path(path).suffix.lower() in self._extensions

    def _first_local_path(self, event) -> str:
        mime = event.mimeData()
        if not mime.hasUrls():
            return ""
        urls = mime.urls()
        if not urls:
            return ""
        return urls[0].toLocalFile()

    def dragEnterEvent(self, event):
        path = self._first_local_path(event)
        if self._is_valid_path(path):
            event.acceptProposedAction()
            self._set_hovered(True)
        else:
            event.ignore()

    def dragMoveEvent(self, event):
        path = self._first_local_path(event)
        if self._is_valid_path(path):
            event.acceptProposedAction()
            self._set_hovered(True)
        else:
            event.ignore()
            self._set_hovered(False)

    def dragLeaveEvent(self, event):
        self._set_hovered(False)
        super().dragLeaveEvent(event)

    def dropEvent(self, event):
        path = self._first_local_path(event)
        self._set_hovered(False)
        if not self._is_valid_path(path):
            event.ignore()
            return
        event.acceptProposedAction()
        logger.info(f"Файл принят в зону: {path}")
        self.file_dropped.emit(path)


======================================================================
# src/pik2video/gui/editor/panels/file_tile.py
======================================================================

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/panels/file_tile.py
import logging
from pathlib import Path

from PySide6.QtCore import QFileInfo, Qt, Signal
from PySide6.QtGui import QPixmap
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QFileIconProvider, QFrame, QLabel, QPushButton, QVBoxLayout

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

BG_NORMAL = "#2a2a2a"
BG_ACTIVE = "#4a4a4a"
BORDER_NORMAL = "#555"
BORDER_ACTIVE = "#8a6f2a"
BG_DIMMED = "#1a1a1a"
BORDER_DIMMED = "#333"

ITEM_ICON_SIZE = 40
ITEM_HEIGHT = 68

class FileTile(QFrame):
    """Универсальная плитка файла: иконка + имя + кнопка удаления."""
    clicked = Signal()
    deleted = Signal()

    def __init__(self, path: str, is_active: bool = False, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._path = path
        self._is_active = is_active
        self._is_dimmed = False

        self.setFixedHeight(ITEM_HEIGHT)
        self.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4)
        layout.setSpacing(2)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)

        self._icon_label = QLabel()
        self._icon_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self._icon_label.setFixedHeight(ITEM_ICON_SIZE)
        self._icon_label.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; border: none;")

        self._show_system_icon()

        self._name_label = QLabel(self._short_name(path))
        self._name_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self._name_label.setWordWrap(True)
        self._name_label.setStyleSheet("color: #ccc; font-size: 9px; background: transparent; border: none;")
        self._name_label.setToolTip(str(path))

        layout.addWidget(self._icon_label)
        layout.addWidget(self._name_label)

        self._delete_btn = QPushButton("🗑️", self)
        self._delete_btn.setFixedSize(18, 18)
        self._delete_btn.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        self._delete_btn.setFocusPolicy(Qt.NoFocus)
        self._delete_btn.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 80);
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 30);
                border-radius: 4px;
                color: #e0e0e0;
                font-size: 10px;
                padding: 0px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background: rgba(200, 50, 50, 150);
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 80, 80, 200);
            }
        """)
        self._delete_btn.clicked.connect(self.deleted.emit)
        self._delete_btn.setVisible(is_active)

        self._apply_style()

    def _short_name(self, path: str) -> str:
        name = Path(path).name
        if len(name) > 14:
            name = name[:11] + "..."
        return name

    def _show_system_icon(self):
        provider = QFileIconProvider()
        icon = provider.icon(QFileInfo(self._path))
        pixmap = icon.pixmap(ITEM_ICON_SIZE, ITEM_ICON_SIZE)
        if not pixmap.isNull():
            self._icon_label.setPixmap(pixmap)

    def _position_delete_btn(self):
        if self._delete_btn is not None:
            self._delete_btn.move(self.width() - 20, 2)

    def resizeEvent(self, event):
        super().resizeEvent(event)
        self._position_delete_btn()

    def _apply_style(self):
        if self._is_active:
            bg, border = BG_ACTIVE, BORDER_ACTIVE
        elif self._is_dimmed:
            bg, border = BG_DIMMED, BORDER_DIMMED
        else:
            bg, border = BG_NORMAL, BORDER_NORMAL

        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            FileTile {{
                background-color: {bg};
                border: 1px solid {border};
                border-radius: 4px;
            }}
        """)

    def set_active(self, active: bool):
        self._is_active = active
        self._apply_style()
        if self._delete_btn is not None:
            self._delete_btn.setVisible(self._is_active and not self._is_dimmed)
            if self._is_active and not self._is_dimmed:
                self._position_delete_btn()
                self._delete_btn.raise_()

    def set_dimmed(self, dimmed: bool):
        self._is_dimmed = dimmed
        self._apply_style()
        if self._delete_btn is not None:
            self._delete_btn.setVisible(self._is_active and not self._is_dimmed)
            if self._is_active and not self._is_dimmed:
                self._position_delete_btn()
                self._delete_btn.raise_()

    def set_thumbnail(self, pixmap: QPixmap):
        if pixmap is None or pixmap.isNull():
            return
        scaled = pixmap.scaled(ITEM_ICON_SIZE, ITEM_ICON_SIZE, Qt.KeepAspectRatio, Qt.SmoothTransformation)
        self._icon_label.setPixmap(scaled)

    def get_path(self) -> str:
        return self._path

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            self.clicked.emit()


======================================================================
# src/pik2video/gui/editor/panels/global_settings_panel.py

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/global_settings_panel.py
"""
Панель глобальных настроек для встраивания в ToolsPanel.

Отвечает только за:
- показ виджетов настроек (язык, размер текста, подсказки, всегда сверху)
- применение изменений в контроллер (сразу, без кнопок)

Не знает:
- про окно-диалог
- про другие страницы ToolsPanel
"""

import logging

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QVBoxLayout, QWidget

from src.pik2video.gui.common.tooltip import set_tooltips_enabled
from src.pik2video.gui.widgets.global_settings import (
    AutoHideEditorSetting,
    LanguageSetting,
    TextSizeSetting,
    TooltipsSetting,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class GlobalSettingsPanel(QWidget):
    """Панель глобальных настроек для встраивания в левую колонку редактора."""

    def __init__(self, controller, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.controller = controller
        self.translator = controller.get_translator()

        self._build_ui()
        self._apply_localization()

        self.translator.language_changed.connect(self._apply_localization)

    def _build_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(0)

        top = QWidget()
        top.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; border: none;")
        top_layout = QVBoxLayout(top)
        top_layout.setContentsMargins(8, 8, 8, 8)
        top_layout.setSpacing(8)

        self.title = QLabel("Настройки")
        self.title.setStyleSheet(
            "color: #888; font-size: 11px; font-weight: bold; "
            "background: transparent; border: none;"
        )
        top_layout.addWidget(self.title)

        self.language_setting = LanguageSetting(self.controller)
        self.text_size_setting = TextSizeSetting(self.controller)
        self.tooltips_setting = TooltipsSetting(self.controller)
        self.auto_hide_setting = AutoHideEditorSetting(self.controller)

        self.row_language = self._make_row(self.language_setting)
        self.row_text_size = self._make_row(self.text_size_setting)
        self.row_tooltips = self._make_row(self.tooltips_setting)
        self.row_auto_hide = self._make_row(self.auto_hide_setting)

        top_layout.addWidget(self.row_language)
        top_layout.addWidget(self.row_text_size)
        top_layout.addWidget(self.row_tooltips)
        top_layout.addWidget(self.row_auto_hide)
        top_layout.addStretch()

        layout.addWidget(top, stretch=1)

        self.language_setting.language_changed.connect(self._on_language_changed)
        self.text_size_setting.size_changed.connect(self._on_text_size_changed)
        self.tooltips_setting.toggled.connect(self._on_tooltips_changed)
        self.auto_hide_setting.toggled.connect(self._on_auto_hide_changed)

    def _make_row(self, control: QWidget) -> QWidget:
        """Компактная строка: label слева, control справа."""
        row = QWidget()
        row.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; border: none;")
        row_layout = QHBoxLayout(row)
        row_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        row_layout.setSpacing(6)

        label = QLabel("")
        label.setWordWrap(True)
        label.setStyleSheet(
            "color: #ccc; font-size: 11px; background: transparent; border: none;"
        )
        row_layout.addWidget(label, stretch=1)
        row_layout.addWidget(control, stretch=0)

        row._label = label
        return row

    def _apply_localization(self):
        """Обновить тексты по текущему языку."""
        self.title.setText(self.translator.tr("settings"))
        self.row_language._label.setText(self.translator.tr("language_setting"))
        self.row_text_size._label.setText(self.translator.tr("text_size_setting"))
        self.row_tooltips._label.setText(self.translator.tr("show_tooltips"))
        self.row_auto_hide._label.setText(self.translator.tr("auto_hide_editor"))


    def _on_language_changed(self, value: str):
        self.controller.set_language(value)
        self.controller.save_settings()

    def _on_text_size_changed(self, value: int):
        self.controller.set_text_size(value)
        self.controller.save_settings()

    def _on_tooltips_changed(self, value: bool):
        self.controller.set_show_tooltips(value)
        self.controller.save_settings()
        set_tooltips_enabled(value)

    def _on_auto_hide_changed(self, value: bool):
        self.controller.set_auto_hide_editor(value)
        self.controller.save_settings()

# src/pik2video/gui/editor/panels/link_button.py
